Need make search robust enough to find below target. Let's design a multi-stage algorithm:

1. Exact fast score function using np.convolve (or FFT) for arrays; normalized to sum1 internally but evaluator clamps. We can avoid scale each time by fixing sum1 for optimization. Evaluator score invariant, but exact evaluator clones list etc.

2. Generate initial profiles:
   - Uniform
   - Exponential one-sided with λ tuned (score ~1.8) to get below2.
   - Two-step asymmetric; optimize step ratio by scanning.
   - Random profiles with simplex constraints.
   - Iteratively construct via "water-filling" / "flat convolution" heuristic: Start with a random sparse or exponential, reduce max peaks by adjusting coefficients.

3. Local optimizers:
   a) Projected subgradient descent on simplex with multiple step sizes and lags selection. Use exact score for acceptance. This can reduce max.
   b) Coordinate pair moves: transfer mass from high gradient to low gradient, with line search by trying a few fractions.
   c) Simulated annealing / randomized projected gradient: occasional random perturbations to escape.
   d) Smooth optimization via L-BFGS on log variables for selected lengths, using approximate smooth max (log-sum-exp) and numpy conv. Then refine with exact acceptance.

- Quick scans for initial sequences (few seconds).

Function `simplex_project(v)`:
```
def simplex_project(v):
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = x.size
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - 1))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    rho = rho[0]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    w = np.clip(x - theta, 0.0, None)
    # ensure sum1 numerical
    s = w.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    # if not close due finite, adjust?
    w += (1.0 - s)/n? But that may reintroduce negative. Better if sum off tiny, renormalize.
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12:
       # try theta small? Or divide
       w = w/w.sum()
    return w
```
Need because v can have large negative, rho formula. Standard `np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - 1.0))[0]` works if 1-indexed. For all negative, rho empty? If sum v negative, largest negative; but after projection need sum1; should always if x finite. Condition might false? Actually if x all -1e9, rho may be 0? Condition u[i]*(i+1) > cumsum-1. For negative, maybe no? Let's use robust water filling.
Alternative simpler: since we often project small perturbations, can do:
```
x = np.clip(v,0,None)
s=x.sum()
if s>0: return x/s
return np.full(n,1.0/n)
```
This is not exact simplex projection (minimizes L2) but valid; may not preserve direction well? It just rescales positives. For gradient descent, using clip+renorm is okay and fast. But can damp moves if many negatives. We can implement exact but complexity O(n log n) per trial; for n=1000 and many trials, okay? Sorting 1000 each trial maybe fine. 1000s * sort 1000 ~1e6 log, OK. Use exact for quality.
We can use clip+renorm for speed and occasional exact? Let's implement exact robust.

Exact projection code (from numpy):
```
def simplex_project(v):
    x = np.asarray(v, float)
    n = x.size
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = (u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - 1.0))
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if len(rho)==0:
       # fallback clip renorm
    else: rho = rho[0]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    w = np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): fallback
    if abs(s-1)>1e-10:
       # due numerical? Could adjust by distributing residual to positive entries
       residual=1-s
       pos=w>0
       if pos.any(): w[pos] += residual/pos.sum()
       if (w<0).any(): w=np.clip(w,0,None); w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
Should be fine.

Convolution score:
```
def convolution_max_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    # normalize not necessary; score invariant but scale can overflow? a values maybe normalized
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    maxb=c.max()
    return 2.0*n*maxb/(s*s)
```
For speed, if array sum1, return 2n*max. But use exact formula.

`score_fast(a)` maybe uses FFT for n>1024. But np.convolve direct maybe simpler. For gradient, need convolution c; use same.

For many iter maybe heavy. Could compute direct with FFT. Let's implement FFT helper for conv and corr when n>256 to speed.

But implementing correct index:
```
def convolve_full_fft(x,y):
   n=len(x); m=len(y)
   N=1
   while N < n+m-1: N*=2
   X=np.fft.rfft(x,N); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
   return np.fft.irfft(X*Y,N)[:n+m-1]
```
Use for c: convolve_full_fft(a,a)[:2n-1].
corr_weights: h=convolve_full_fft(a, w[::-1]) length n+L-1 (L=2n-1) =3n-2. Need indices L-1-j? L is w len. t = L-1-j (0..n-1). Return h[t]. Note h index from 0. Good.

- Work with normalized sum1.
- Maintain `a` and `score_fast` (approx) and maybe exact score for best within.
- Use local best separate from global? We'll have global best updated at end of each thread? Single thread.
- Iteration:
   * compute c=convolve(a,a) (maybe cached)
   * M=c.max()
   * Choose top lags: `active = np.where(c >= M - thresh)[0]`; if count small (<max(8, n//100)), use top K percentile.
   * Options for weights:
       - uniform over active: w = zeros, w[active]=1/len(active)
       - smooth: z=c-M; tau = max(1e-12, 0.1*M); w=exp(z/tau); normalize.
   * g = 2*corr_weights(a,w)
   * Direction d = g - mean(g) (or simply projected gradient from pushing down high g). But to keep sum, use gradient projected: d = g - g.mean(). However for nonnegative, we want decrease components with high g. Since g nonnegative, subtracting d where d positive for high g and negative for low? d = g - mean(g); low g may be negative, adding increases low. But if all g positive, d negative for low? With a - step*d? If gradient F increases with a in high g directions, descent direction is -g. To project to sum, descent direction = -g + mean(g) (since sum zero). So move a += step * (mean(g) - g). Let's use descent_dir = -g + np.mean(g). Then a_trial = a + alpha*descent_dir (not a - alpha*g + mean). Project simplex.

Detailed local_search algorithm:
```
def local_search(a, init_score, deadline, rng, max_iterations=None, verbose=False):
    n=a.size
    a = np.clip(a,0,None); a/=a.sum()
    best_local=a.copy(); best_local_score=init_score
    # maybe exact best score using score_array
    c = convolve_self(a)
    it=0
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       M = c.max()
       # select active lags
       # threshold: include c >= M - 0.08*M, but at least top 5%, at most 80%
       thresh = M - 0.10*M
       active = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
       if len(active) < max(8, n//50):
           q = int(np.ceil(0.05 * len(c)))
           active = np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]
       if len(active) > int(0.8*len(c)):
           q = int(0.8*len(c))
           active = np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[active]=1.0/len(active)
       g = gradient_correlation(a,w)  # includes 2? We'll not halve; direction uses g.
       d = -g + g.mean()
       # randomize direction occasionally: add small random
       if rng.random()<0.1:
           d += 0.01 * (rng.random(n)-0.5) * np.mean(a)
       # normalize direction L1
       l1=np.sum(np.abs(d))
       if l1 <= 1e-14: break
       d/=l1
       # candidate alphas relative to 1 (L1)
       alphas = [1e-4, 1e-3, 1e-2, 5e-2, 0.2, 0.8, 2.0]
       # Maybe scale by 1/n? Since total L1=1, adding 0.2 large. Use decreasing.
       best_trial=None; best_trial_score=best_local_score
       for alpha in alphas:
           v = a + alpha*d
           # Project to simplex
           tr = simplex_project(v)
           # maybe quick score fast
           sc = score_array(tr)
           if sc < best_trial_score:
               best_trial_score=sc; best_trial=tr
       if best_trial is not None and np.isfinite(best_trial_score):
           # Accept, update a, c
           a=best_trial.copy()
           if best_trial_score < best_local_score - 1e-12:
               best_local=a.copy(); best_local_score=best_trial_score; stall=0
               if verbose and best_local_score<1.55: print...
           else: stall +=1
           c=convolve_self(a)
           # optionally random block perturbation every 200 iters
           if it%200==0 and (best_local_score>1.51 or stall>50):
               cand=block_perturb(a,rng)
               sc=score_array(cand)
               if sc<best_local_score: a=cand; best_local=a; best_local_score=sc; c=convolve_self(a)
       else:
           stall +=1
           # maybe try pure random pair moves
           if stall > 20:
              ...
           # reduce alphas? We can change sign? If direction fails, try negative? But descent direction should. Add random.
       it+=1
    return best_local, best_local_score
```

Implement LP stage:
```
def lp_proposal(f, max_time=20):
   n=f.size
   c=convolve_self(f); M=c.max()
   # build A: for each k 0..2n-2, banded coefficients f_i
   rows = []; cols=[]; data=[]
   for k in range(2*n-1):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       for i in range(lo,hi+1): rows.append(k); cols.append(i); data.append(f[i])
   A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2n-1,n))
   from scipy.optimize import linprog
   res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':max_time, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8,...})
   if res.status in (0,3): return res.x
```
Need import scipy inside? We can import top-level? Allowed. To avoid dependency if not available? SciPy available. Use try/except.
But note if M small, A entries small, B small; f satisfies A f = f*f ≤M. Sum f=1. LP objective could give sum maybe finite.
Time: Presolve plus solve. Use n ≤ 256 for LP stage. Several iterations.

```
def _fft_conv(x,y):
    size = 1
    need = len(x)+len(y)-1
    while size < need: size <<=1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x, size)
    Y=np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(X*Y, size)
    return out[:need]
```
For many calls, computing power each time overhead. Could precompute size? Not easily in top-level without state. Fine.

Block perturbation for nonnegative sum: choose random interval width relative n, multiply by exp(N(0,sigma)), or transfer mass. Use several variations:
```
def random_perturbation(a, rng):
   n=a.size
   b=a.copy()
   width=int(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5)))? bounded
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
   factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0, rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5])))
   b[start:start+width]*=factor
   # maybe add random small noise all: b *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma,n)); sigma chosen
   b=np.clip(b,0,None); if sum=0: return uniform; b/=b.sum()
   return b
```
Also pair transfer using gradient:
```
def pair_transfer(a, g, rng, frac):
   i=argmax g where a_i>small; j=argmin g where a_j>0
   move = frac*a[i]
   a[i]-=move; a[j]+=move; if a[i]<0 move adjust; normalize?
```
Since sum preserved, no need normalize. Use fracs [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3]. Good.

```
def lp_mix(f, seconds=30):
   try import scipy.optimize, scipy.sparse
   for _ in range(3):
      g = solve LP for f
      if g is None: break
      g = g/g.sum()
      for lam in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
         mix=(1-lam)*f+lam*g; sc=score_array(mix)
         if sc<best: f=mix
   return f,best
```

Implement `internal_score` direct only:
```
def internal_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   # clamp? we keep nonneg; but for safety
   if (a<0).any(): a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum()
   if s==0: return inf
   # Scale invariance; to avoid overflow, normalize by s
   b=a/s
   c=np.convolve(b,b)  # direct
   return 2.0*n*c.max()
```
Since normalized sum1, score=2n*max. This avoids sum^2. It matches evaluator after clamping: evaluator score=2n*max_b/sum^2 with original; if b=sum1, sum_b=1, max_b = max a*a /s^2, so same. Good. Values small. Use direct np.convolve; for n=2000 4M, okay. c of normalized sum1: entries ~1/n. Good.

`make_power_sequence(n, p, shift, gamma, beta, amp, phase)`:
```
i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
x=i/(max(1,n-1))
a=(i+shift)**(-p)
a *= np.exp(-gamma*x)
# optional center Gaussian boost/dip
if amp: a *= (1 + amp*np.exp(-((x-cen)/width)**2))
# optional sinusoid

a=np.maximum(a,0); normalize
```

Fast convolution with normalized a:
```
def fast_conv_self(a):
   n=a.size
   size=1
   need=2*n-1
   while size<need: size*=2
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:need]
   # Small negative due fft
   return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
For n small, direct maybe. Use direct if n<128.


Active weights:
```
def make_weights(c, rng):
   L=len(c)
   M=c.max()
   if M<=0: uniform? return np.full(L,1/L)
   mode=rng.integers(0,3)
   if mode==0:
       q=max(8,int(0.06*L)); active=np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]; w zeros; w[active]=1/q; return
   if mode==1:
       q=max(16,int(0.25*L)); active=...; w...
   else:
       tau=max(1e-15, 0.08*M)
       z=(c-M)/tau
       # avoid underflow; z<=0
       w=np.exp(z)
       s=w.sum(); if not finite: top 25
       w/=s; return
```

```
cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1.0)
idx = np.nonzero(cond)[0]

theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
w=np.maximum(x-theta,0)
s=w.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: fallback clip
if abs(s-1)>1e-12:
   # distribute residual to positive; if too large, renorm
   residual=1-s
   pos=w>0
   if pos.any() and abs(residual)<s:
       w[pos]+=residual/pos.sum()
       w=np.clip(w,0,None)
   if w.sum()<=0: fallback uniform
   else: w/=w.sum()
return w
```

`downsample`: average blocks to target. If parent length smaller, maybe resample by linear? Use `np.interp`.

```
def downsample_sequence(a, target):
   if a.size == target: return a
   if a.size < target:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,target); return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   # block average
   new=np.empty(target); for i in range(target): start=int(i*a.size/target); end=max(start+1,int((i+1)*a.size/target)); new[i]=a[start:end].mean()
   normalize
```
Loop O(n) okay.

```
def pair_transfer_candidate(a,g,rng):
   b=a.copy()
   high = np.flatnonzero(g >= np.quantile(g,0.95))
   low = np.flatnonzero(g <= np.quantile(g,0.05))
   if len(high)==0: high=np.arange(n); if low empty: low
   i=int(rng.choice(high)); j=int(rng.choice(low))
   frac=float(rng.choice([0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3]))
   amt=frac*b[i]
   if amt> b[i]: amt=b[i]*0.9
   b[i]-=amt; b[j]+=amt; if b[i]<0: b[np.argmin(b)]...; normalize
```

Let's write `make_weights` with no lambda.

```
def make_weights(c, rng):
    L=c.size
    M=float(c.max())
    if M <= 0: return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,3))
    if mode==0:
       q=max(16, int(0.08*L))
       part=np.argpartition(c, -q)
       part=part[-q:]
    elif mode==1:
       q=max(32, int(0.30*L))
       ...
    else:
       tau=max(1e-15, 0.08*M)
       z=(c-M)/tau
       exp_z=np.exp(z)
       s=exp_z.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
           q=max(32, int(0.2*L)); ...
       else:
           return exp_z/s
    w=np.zeros(L); w[part]=1.0/len(part); return w
```
`np.argpartition(c, -q)` requires q between 1 and L-1? If q>=L, set q=L-1? Use `q = min(q, max(1, L-1))`? If q=L, argpartition with -L? It may error. Use `q = max(1, min(L-1, q))` but if L=1 handle. For L small, q=1.
Better: `q = int(np.ceil(frac*L))`; `q = max(1, min(L, q))`; if q==L, active all. For argpartition, use `partition = np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]`; if q==L, -L = -L maybe works? It expects kth between 0 and n-1. Use if q>=L: `part=np.arange(L)`. Else argpartition.

`make_power_sequence` parameters: Let's finalize signature:
```
def make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.5, gamma=0.0, right_p=0.0, right_shift=0.5, amp=0.0, center=0.5, width=0.2, sin_amp=0.0, freq=1.0, phase=0.0):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    den=max(1,n-1)
    x=i/den
    base=(i+float(shift))**(-float(p))
    if right_p:
        base *= (n-1.0-i+float(right_shift))**(-float(right_p))
    if gamma:
        base *= np.exp(-float(gamma)*x)
    if amp:
        base *= 1.0 + float(amp)*np.exp(-((x-float(center))/float(width))**2)
    if sin_amp:
        base *= 1.0 + float(sin_amp)*np.sin(2*np.pi*float(freq)*x + float(phase))
    base=np.clip(base,0.0,None)
    s=base.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return base/s
```
Ensure width>1e-6. If sin factor becomes negative for large amp; amp limited random [ -0.4,0.4] so positive. If deterministic p maybe.

If n=1, right index n-1-i =0; right_p base factor shift. x=0.

```
right_p_list=[0,0.2,0.5]
```

```
   for other in selected[:3]:

          mix=0.5*best+0.5*other; local_search short deadline min(120?)
   else if different sizes? skip.
```

```
def local_search(a, hint_score, deadline, rng, verbose=False):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return...
    a=a/s
    current_exact=exact_score(a)
    if hint_score is not None and finite and hint_score < current_exact: current_exact=hint_score # maybe fast lower, no; use exact
    best_local=a.copy(); best_score=current_exact
    stall=0; it=0; last_gen= np.max? 
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       c=fast_conv_self(a)
       w=make_weights(c,rng)
       g=fast_corr_weights(a,w)
       d=-g+g.mean()
       if rng.random()<0.1: d += (rng.standard_normal(a.size)-rng.standard_normal(a.size).mean()) * 0.05
       l1=np.sum(np.abs(d))
       if l1<1e-15:
           stall += 1
       else:
           d/=l1
           alphas=(1e-4,1e-3,1e-2,5e-2,0.2,0.7)
           # Maybe adapt: if n<256 include 1.5
           trial_scores=[]; trial_pages=[]
           for alpha in alphas:
              tr=simplex_project(a + alpha*d)
              fs=fast_score(tr)
              if np.isfinite(fs): ...
           if trial_scores:
              fs_arr=np.array(trial_scores); order=np.argsort(fs_arr)
              exact_trials = 1 if n>2048 else 2
              accepted=False
              for pos in order[:exact_trials]:
                  ex=exact_score(trial_seqs[pos])
                  if ex < current_exact - 1e-14:
                      a=trial_seqs[pos]; current_exact=ex; if ex<best_score: best...; stall=0; accepted=True; break
              if accepted:
                  it+=1; continue
       # no accept
       stall += 1
       it += 1
       if stall > 25:
          # random perturbations
          improved=False
          for _ in range(12):
              rnd=random_perturbation(a,rng,g)
              fs=fast_score(rnd)
              if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < current_exact*1.02:
                  ex=exact_score(rnd)
                  if ex < current_exact - 1e-12:
                     a=rnd; current_exact=ex; best...; improved=True; break
          if improved: stall=5
       if stall > 300: break
    return best_local,best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Use the given parent sequence if available, plus a broad set of fast
  parametric/random starts (endpoint-singular power profiles, tapers,
  asymmetries, sinusoidal/gaussian buffers).
- Keep several of the best lengths and refine with a projected-subgradient
  local search on the simplex.  The subgradient uses weighted max
  convolution lags and the exact evaluator is used for accept/reject.
- Occasionally apply random perturbations/pair transfers to escape basins.
- Use the LP/SOTA-style mixing heuristic when the sequence is short enough:
  for current f with M=max(f*f), solve max sum(g) s.t. g>=0, f*g<=M, then
  mix toward the rescaled extreme point g.
- Return the best valid sequence found before the budget expires.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_pow2(n):
    n = int(n)
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0), 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size)
    return a / s


def exact_score(a):
    """Authoritative-like direct score, scale-invariant."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def evaluator_score(a):
    """Call the official evaluator if available; otherwise use exact_score."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return exact_score(a)


def consider_candidate(candidate, best_seq, best_score):
    candidate = np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if candidate.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(candidate).all():
        return best_seq, best_score
    candidate = np.clip(candidate, 0.0, None)
    s = float(candidate.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return best_seq, best_score
    candidate = candidate / s
    sc = evaluator_score(candidate)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return candidate.copy(), sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def fast_conv_self(a):
    """FFT-based self-convolution (clipped to nonnegative)."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([])
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 128:
        out = np.convolve(a, a)
        return np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    size = next_pow2(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:need]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def fast_score(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv_self(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def fast_corr_weights(a, w):
    """
    For a length n and weights w length 2n-1, return
        g[j] = sum_m a[m] * w[j+m]
    used for the subgradient of selected convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([])
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    need = n + L - 1
    if n <= 128:
        out = np.convolve(a, w[::-1])
        return out[L - 1 - np.arange(n)]
    size = next_pow2(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, size)[:need]
    return out[L - 1 - np.arange(n)]


def simplex_project(v):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1}."""
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([])
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)

    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    idx = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        rho = n - 1
    else:
        rho = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    w = np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
        return w / s
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        pos = w > 0.0
        residual = 1.0 - s
        if pos.any() and abs(residual) < 1.0:
            w[pos] += residual / float(pos.sum())
            w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
            s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
        w = w / s
    return w


def downsample_sequence(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    target = int(target)
    if a.size <= 1 or target <= 1:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == target:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size < target:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target)
        b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    else:
        b = np.empty(target, dtype=np.float64)
        m = a.size
        for i in range(target):
            lo = int(i * m / target)
            hi = int((i + 1) * m / target)
            if hi <= lo:
                hi = lo + 1
            b[i] = float(a[lo:hi].mean())
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target)
    return b / s


def upsample_sequence(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    target = int(target)
    if a.size <= 0:
        return np.full(max(1, target), 1.0 / max(1, target))
    if a.size == target:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(target, 1.0 / target)
    return b / s


def make_power_sequence(
    n,
    p=0.5,
    shift=0.5,
    gamma=0.0,
    right_p=0.0,
    right_shift=0.5,
    amp=0.0,
    center=0.5,
    width=0.2,
    sin_amp=0.0,
    freq=1.0,
    phase=0.0,
):
    """
    General nonnegative profile:
        (i+shift)^-p * (n-1-i+right_shift)^-right_p
        * exp(-gamma*x) * (1 + amp*gauss) * (1 + sin_amp*sin)
    """
    n = max(1, int(n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    den = max(1, n - 1)
    x = i / float(den)

    try:
        p = max(0.0, float(p))
        shift = max(1e-3, float(shift))
        gamma = max(0.0, float(gamma))
        right_p = max(0.0, float(right_p))
        right_shift = max(1e-3, float(right_shift))
        amp = float(amp)
        center = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(center)))
        width = max(0.02, float(width))
        sin_amp = float(sin_amp)
        freq = max(0.1, float(freq))
        phase = float(phase)
    except Exception:
        p = 0.5
        shift = 0.5
        gamma = 0.0
        right_p = 0.0
        right_shift = 0.5
        amp = 0.0
        center = 0.5
        width = 0.2
        sin_amp = 0.0
        freq = 1.0
        phase = 0.0

    a = np.power(i + shift, -p)

    if right_p > 0.0:
        a = a * np.power(n - 1.0 - i + right_shift, -right_p)
    if gamma > 0.0:
        a = a * np.exp(-gamma * x)
    if amp != 0.0:
        a = a * (1.0 + amp * np.exp(-(((x - center) / width) ** 2)))
    if sin_amp != 0.0:
        a = a * (1.0 + sin_amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return a / s


def random_perturbation(a, rng, g=None):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return a.copy()

    b = a.copy()

    if g is not None and rng.random() < 0.45:
        if n >= 16:
            hi_thr = float(np.quantile(g, 0.95))
            lo_thr = float(np.quantile(g, 0.05))
            hi = np.flatnonzero(g >= hi_thr)
            lo = np.flatnonzero(g <= lo_thr)
            if hi.size == 0:
                hi = np.arange(n)
            if lo.size == 0:
                lo = np.arange(n)
            i = int(hi[int(rng.integers(0, hi.size))])
            j = int(lo[int(rng.integers(0, lo.size))])
        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i == j:
                j = (j + 1) % n
        frac = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.2])))
        amt = frac * b[i]
        if amt > b[i]:
            amt = 0.95 * b[i]
        if amt > 0.0:
            b[i] -= amt
            b[j] += amt
    else:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
        if mode == 0:
            sigma = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.35])))
            b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n))
        elif mode == 1:
            width = max(1, int(n * float(rng.choice(np.array([0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.5])))))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.choice(np.array([0.08, 0.15, 0.3, 0.7]))))))
            b[start:stop] *= factor
        else:
            k = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            fraction = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.2])))
            amt = fraction / float(n)
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                donor = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if b[donor] > amt:
                    b[donor] -= amt
                    b[k] += amt
            else:
                add = amt
                b[k] += add
                b *= 1.0 / float(b.sum() / 1.0)

    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return b / s


def make_weights(c, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([])
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L)

    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))

    if mode == 0:
        frac = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.05, 0.08, 0.12])))
    elif mode == 1:
        frac = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.2, 0.3, 0.45])))
    else:
        tau = max(1e-15, 0.08 * M)
        z = (c - M) / tau
        w = np.exp(z)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            return w / s
        frac = 0.25

    q = int(np.ceil(frac * L))
    q = max(1, min(L, q))
    if q >= L:
        part = np.arange(L)
    else:
        part = np.argpartition(c, -q)[-q:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[part] = 1.0 / float(part.size)
    return w


def local_search(a, hint_score, deadline, rng):
    """
    Projected weighted-subgradient descent on the simplex, with exact
    evaluation for accept/reject and random escape moves.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 2:
        sc = exact_score(a)
        return a, sc

    current_exact = exact_score(a)
    if (hint_score is not None) and np.isfinite(hint_score) and hint_score < current_exact:
        # hint may be a fast score; still keep exact as authoritative.
        pass
    best_local = a.copy()
    best_local_exact = current_exact

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    alphas = np.array([1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 0.1, 0.35])

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

        c = fast_conv_self(a)
        w = make_weights(c, rng)
        g = fast_corr_weights(a, w)
        d = -g + float(g.mean())

        if rng.random() < 0.12:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            noise = noise - noise.mean()
            d = d + 0.08 * noise

        l1 = float(np.sum(np.abs(d)))
        if (not np.isfinite(l1)) or l1 < 1e-16:
            stall += 1
            it += 1
            if stall > 300:
                break
            continue

        d /= l1
        accepted = False

        trial_scores = []
        trial_seqs = []

        for alpha in alphas:
            trial = simplex_project(a + float(alpha) * d)
            fs = fast_score(trial)
            if np.isfinite(fs):
                trial_scores.append(fs)
                trial_seqs.append(trial)

        if trial_scores:
            fs_arr = np.asarray(trial_scores, dtype=np.float64)
            order = np.argsort(fs_arr)
            exact_trials = 1 if n > 2048 else 2
            for pos in order[:max(1, exact_trials)]:
                trial = trial_seqs[int(pos)]
                ex = exact_score(trial)
                if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < current_exact - 1e-13:
                    a = trial
                    current_exact = ex
                    if ex < best_local_exact:
                        best_local = a.copy()
                        best_local_exact = ex
                        stall = 0
                    else:
                        stall = min(stall, 10)
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if accepted:
            it += 1
            continue

        stall += 1
        it += 1

        if stall > 25:
            improved = False
            for _ in range(14):
                rnd = random_perturbation(a, rng, g)
                fs = fast_score(rnd)
                if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < max(1e-300, current_exact * 1.03):
                    ex = exact_score(rnd)
                    if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < current_exact - 1e-12:
                        a = rnd
                        current_exact = ex
                        if ex < best_local_exact:
                            best_local = a.copy()
                            best_local_exact = ex
                        improved = True
                        break
            if improved:
                stall = 5

        if stall > 350:
            break

    return best_local, best_local_exact


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP/SOTA-style mixing
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def lp_solve(f, remaining):
    """
    Given f>=0 with sum 1 and M=max(f*f), solve
        max sum(g)
        s.t. g>=0,
             (f*g)[k] <= M, for all k.
    Returns normalized g, or None.
    """
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
        from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    except Exception:
        return None

    f = normalize_nonnegative(f)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n <= 2 or n > 768:
        return None

    c = fast_conv_self(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    M = M * 1.000001

    rows = []
    cols = []
    vals = []

    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
            cnt = int(idx.size)
            rows.extend([k] * cnt)
            cols.extend(idx.tolist())
            vals.extend(f[lo:hi + 1].tolist())

    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.asarray(cols, dtype=np.int32)
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)

    A = csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    tlim = max(3.0, float(remaining) * 0.35)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": tlim, "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return g / s


def lp_refine(candidate, deadline):
    candidate = normalize_nonnegative(candidate)
    n = int(candidate.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return candidate, exact_score(candidate)

    if n > 512:
        work = downsample_sequence(candidate, 512)
    else:
        work = candidate.copy()

    work_score = exact_score(work)
    best_work = work.copy()
    best_score = work_score
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    for _ in range(10):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if rem < 3.0:
            break
        g = lp_solve(best_work, rem)
        if g is None:
            break
        improved = False
        for lam in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            mix = (1.0 - lam) * best_work + lam * g
            mix = normalize_nonnegative(mix)
            sc = exact_score(mix)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_work = mix
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            break

    if n > 512:
        cand = upsample_sequence(best_work, n)
    else:
        cand = best_work

    return cand, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Start generation
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _add_start(cands, seq):
    seq = normalize_nonnegative(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return
    fs = fast_score(seq)
    if np.isfinite(fs):
        cands.append((float(fs), seq))


def generate_initial_candidates(rng, deadline):
    cands = []

    # Parent sequence, if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and arr.size > 0:
                parent = arr / s
            else:
                parent = None
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        for target in (256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            _add_start(cands, downsample_sequence(parent, target))

    lengths = (128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096)

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        # Deterministic strong starts.
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.5))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=1.0))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=1.5))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.5, gamma=0.35))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.5, gamma=0.8))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.45, shift=0.8))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.55, shift=0.8))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.8, right_p=0.25, right_shift=1.2))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.5, shift=0.8, right_p=0.5, right_shift=1.2, gamma=0.4))
        _add_start(cands, make_power_sequence(n, p=0.4, shift=0.8, gamma=0.5, amp=0.35, center=0.35, width=0.25))

        if n <= 256:
            count = 50
        elif n <= 512:
            count = 30
        elif n <= 1024:
            count = 20
        elif n <= 2048:
            count = 12
        else:
            count = 8

        for _ in range(count):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            p = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.70))
            shift = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0])))
            gamma = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.2))
            right_p = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.55)) if rng.random() < 0.75 else 0.0
            right_shift = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8])))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(-0.45, 0.95))
            center = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            width = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.55))
            sin_amp = float(rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25))
            freq = float(rng.choice(np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0])))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))

            _add_start(
                cands,
                make_power_sequence(
                    n,
                    p=p,
                    shift=shift,
                    gamma=gamma,
                    right_p=right_p,
                    right_shift=right_shift,
                    amp=amp,
                    center=center,
                    width=width,
                    sin_amp=sin_amp,
                    freq=freq,
                    phase=phase,
                ),
            )

        # A little pure randomness for diversity.
        if n <= 1024:
            for _ in range(4):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                alpha = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.35, 0.8, 1.5, 3.0])))
                v = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha))
                _add_start(cands, v)

    return cands


def select_search_starts(cands, deadline):
    if not cands:
        return []

    fs_arr = np.asarray([x for x, _ in cands], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(fs_arr)

    exact_cands = []
    budget = 0
    for pos in order:
        if budget >= 70 or time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        _, seq = cands[int(pos)]
        sc = exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            exact_cands.append((float(sc), seq))
        budget += 1

    if not exact_cands:
        return []

    e_arr = np.asarray([x for x, _ in exact_cands], dtype=np.float64)
    eorder = np.argsort(e_arr)

    sorted_exact = [exact_cands[int(i)] for i in eorder]

    chosen = []
    seen = set()
    for sc, seq in sorted_exact:
        n = int(seq.size)
        if n not in seen:
            chosen.append(seq)
            seen.add(n)
            if len(chosen) >= 5:
                break

    # Make sure the single best exact start is included.
    if exact_cands and eorder.size > 0:
        best_seq = sorted_exact[0][1]
        best_n = int(best_seq.size)
        ok = False
        for seq in chosen:
            if int(seq.size) == best_n:
                ok = True
                break
        if not ok and len(chosen) < 6:
            chosen.append(best_seq)

    return chosen


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main program
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Returns the best nonnegative sequence found before budget_s expires."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        B = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        B = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(B) or B <= 0.0:
        B = 60.0
    total_T = min(B, 1800.0)

    if seed is None:
        seed = 12345
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Time allocations.
    t_gen = t_start + max(10.0, 0.15 * total_T)
    t_local = t_start + max(t_gen + 30.0, 0.78 * total_T)
    t_lp = t_start + max(t_local + 10.0, 0.92 * total_T)
    t_end = t_start + max(60.0, total_T - 4.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Generate and rank starts.
    cands = generate_initial_candidates(rng, min(t_gen, t_end - 30.0))
    starts = select_search_starts(cands, t_gen)

    if not starts:
        starts = [make_power_sequence(256, p=0.5, shift=0.5)]

    # ICE fallback default.
    default_seq = make_power_sequence(256, p=0.5, shift=0.5)
    default_score = exact_score(default_seq)

    # Refresh global best from starts.
    for seq in starts:
        best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(seq, best_seq, best_score)

    if best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = default_seq.copy()
        best_score = default_score
        print("Initial best fallback score:", best_score, flush=True)
    else:
        print("Initial best score:", best_score, "length", int(best_seq.size), flush=True)

    # Local search over selected starts.
    if time.monotonic() < t_local - 5.0:
        n_starts = len(starts)
        for i, seq in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() >= t_local - 5.0:
                break
            phase_start = t_gen
            phase_end = t_local
            frac_end = (i + 1) / float(n_starts)
            dl = min(t_local - 3.0, phase_start + (phase_end - phase_start) * frac_end - 3.0)
            if dl <= time.monotonic() + 3.0:
                continue
            print(
                "Local search",
                i + 1,
                "of",
                n_starts,
                "length",
                int(seq.size),
                "deadline in",
                float(dl - time.monotonic()),
                "s",
                flush=True,
            )
            loc_seq, loc_score = local_search(seq, fast_score(seq), dl, rng)
            if loc_seq is not None and loc_seq.size > 0:
                best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(loc_seq, best_seq, best_score)
                print(
                    "  local returned exact score",
                    float(loc_score),
                    "global best",
                    float(best_score),
                    flush=True,
                )

    # LP refinement if short enough or by downsample/upsample.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < t_lp - 10.0:
        tried = False
        if int(best_seq.size) <= 512:
            work, wsc = lp_refine(best_seq, min(t_lp - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0))
            if work is not None:
                best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(work, best_seq, best_score)
                print("LP (same length) score", float(best_score), flush=True)
                tried = True
        else:
            # Try LP on a 512-point downsample, then upsample.
            ds = downsample_sequence(best_seq, 512)
            work, wsc = lp_refine(ds, min(t_lp - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0))
            if work is not None:
                up = upsample_sequence(work, int(best_seq.size))
                best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(up, best_seq, best_score)
                print("LP (down/upsample) score", float(best_score), flush=True)
                tried = True
        if not tried:
            pass

    # Final focused local search from current best.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < t_end - 10.0:
        dl = t_end - 6.0
        print(
            "Final local search from global best, remaining",
            float(dl - time.monotonic()),
            "s",
            flush=True,
        )
        loc_seq, loc_score = local_search(best_seq, fast_score(best_seq), dl, rng)
        if loc_seq is not None:
            best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(loc_seq, best_seq, best_score)
        print("Final best score:", float(best_score), "length", int(best_seq.size) if best_seq is not None else 0, flush=True)

    # If we have some time, mix with another strong start of same length.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < t_end - 30.0 and starts:
        bn = int(best_seq.size)
        dl = t_end - 8.0
        for seq in starts[:4]:
            if time.monotonic() >= dl - 8.0:
                break
            if int(seq.size) == bn:
                mix = 0.5 * best_seq + 0.5 * seq
                mix = normalize_nonnegative(mix)
                loc_seq, loc_score = local_search(mix, fast_score(mix), dl, rng)
                if loc_seq is not None:
                    best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(loc_seq, best_seq, best_score)
                    print("Mix local best score:", float(best_score), flush=True)
            else:
                # Downsample/mix to same size and low overhead.
                mix = 0.6 * best_seq + 0.4 * downsample_sequence(seq, bn)
                mix = normalize_nonnegative(mix)
                if dl - time.monotonic() > 8.0:
                    loc_seq, loc_score = local_search(mix, fast_score(mix), min(dl, time.monotonic() + 90.0), rng)
                    if loc_seq is not None:
                        best_seq, best_score = consider_candidate(loc_seq, best_seq, best_score)

    # Guarantee a valid return.
    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = default_seq.copy()

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, None)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        best_seq = np.full(max(1, best_seq.size), 1.0 / max(1, best_seq.size))
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / s

    final_score = evaluator_score(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_score = exact_score(best_seq)
    print("Returned sequence score:", float(final_score), "length", int(best_seq.size), flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
